- [`absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/README.md)

### proofs

| lean name | proof title | applicable to topics |
|---|---|---|
| [`digamma_conj`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L30) | Schwarz reflection for the digamma function, derived from Complex.Gamma_conj. | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`arch_contour_identity`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L55) | The contour identity. The CCM archimedean symbol on the real line is minus one half of the Weil factor continued to Im = 1/2: Re A_arch(ξ… | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`archSymbol_even`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L75) | The archimedean symbol is even: A(-ξ) = A(ξ). | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`weilOmega_neg_conj`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L88) | The Weil archimedean factor satisfies Ω(-z) = Ω(conj z). | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`symbol_conj`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L102) | Conjugation symmetry of the symbol A_arch(s) - ζ'/ζ(s) = -Z'/Z(s). | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`symbol_conj_critical_line`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L139) | The symbol conjugation on the critical line: M(1/2 - it) = conj (M(1/2 + it)). | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`analyticAt_Gamma_of_re_pos`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L159) | Γ is analytic at every point of the open right half-plane. | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`differentiableAt_digamma_of_re_pos`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L179) | The digamma function is differentiable on the open right half-plane. | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`continuousAt_digamma_of_re_pos`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L197) | The digamma function is continuous on the open right half-plane. | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`digamma_add_conj_critical`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L204) | Digamma sum at conjugate points on the critical line: ψ(1/4+it/2) + ψ(1/4−it/2) = 2·Re ψ(1/4+it/2). | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |
| [`archimedeanTerm_critical_line_real`](../absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Archimedean.lean#L216) | The archimedean term on the critical line is real: (1/2)log π − (1/2)(ψ(1/4+it/2)+ψ(1/4−it/2)) is a real number. | absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization |

| [`etaPartial_even_pos`](../harmonic-sine-eta/lean/Topic.lean#L145) | Even truncations of the eta series are strictly positive for positive exponent and at least one pair of terms. | harmonic-sine-eta |
